# es-foral-payroll: figure and rule ledger
Frozen Guide SHA256 `4eb5391c1aaff726187687bdbad8eb04ba86ae4ccd94ac3d3b3f0772409f164f`; HEAD0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641. Writer evidence, not independent review or accountant attestation. Source quotations retain original language with whitespace joined; case figures are computed, not quoted statutory amounts. All source bodies are reused by exact identity, not silently replaced.
| # | Guide section and native LF | Figure / rule as written | Source act/article | Official URL | Exact short source quote | Actual read date | Type / scope |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Who this applies to; LF26 | [Basque Economic Agreement art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2002-9969#a7) assigns ordinary employment withholding by where work/services are performed. Where common/Basque work is mixed or its location cannot be determined, use the assigned work centre; telework, foreign work and the specified sea-platform work follow that centre. Apply the competent Basque territory's rules, rather than the employee's annual IRPF residence alone. Pensions/public-system benefits generally follow recipient residence. Directors have payer-domicile and joint corporate-tax allocation rules. State public-sector pay has its separate State allocation and listed institutional exceptions. An employee living in Madrid but assigned to a Bilbao centre for telework does not acquire the State payroll algorithm simply from that residence. | Economic Agreement article 7 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2002-9969#a7 | En el supuesto de que los trabajos o servicios se presten en territorio común y vasco o no se pueda determinar el lugar en donde se realicen los trabajos o servicios, se considerará que los trabajos o servicios se prestan en el territorio donde se ubique el centro de trabajo al que esté adscrita la persona trabajadora. | 2026-10-28 | rule |
| 2 | Who this applies to; LF28 | [Navarra Economic Agreement art. 10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1990-31117#a1-13) similarly assigns ordinary work in Navarra, mixed/indeterminate work by assigned centre and telework/foreign work to that centre. It separately assigns Navarra/local public employees, route transport workers by payer domicile, pensions and directors under their specified limbs, with State public-sector exceptions and compensation provisions. Keep the final annual-residence tax calculation and the withholding administration as separate determinations. Obtain actual work, centre-assignment, payer and residence records. | Economic Agreement article 10 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1990-31117#a1-13 | En el supuesto de que los trabajos o servicios se presten en territorio común y navarro, o no se pueda determinar el lugar donde se realicen los trabajos o servicios, se considerará que los mismos se prestan en Navarra cuando se ubique en este territorio el centro de trabajo al que esté adscrita la persona trabajadora. | 2026-10-28 | rule |

| 173 | The base / Basque specific employee/pension inputs; LF109 | For all three current regulations, a newly acquired pension/passive-benefit status uses **annualisation** for selecting the rate, with the statutory option not to annualise. Do not apply the older Álava FAQ's one-child pension column to current 2026 law. Sporadic daily manual labour uses **daily pay ×100** for the table forecast. A judicial spouse/partner compensatory pension can reduce the forecast under the specific local rule, with a **2% rate floor**, an indefinite contract, the first eligible tax period being the one after the court fixed it, and the required communication/judgment evidence. Gipuzkoa additionally states the recipient must not cohabit with the payer. Do not confuse a child's maintenance payments with this spouse-pension route. Special fishing, elected impatriate and founder/foreign-tax-credit routes require their separate statutory conditions and are outside this ordinary table method ([B art. 88.3](https://www.bizkaia.eus/documents/880307/15187815/ca_47_2014.pdf), [A art. 84.3](https://web.araba.eus/documents/d/araba/indice-reglamento_irpf_cas-2-pdf), [G art. 100.3](https://www.gipuzkoa.eus/documents/2456431/8.0755457E7/DF%2033-2014%20(2026-2).pdf/7e4c25eb-a139-fa5f-d310-deaa4c4bcffd)). | Own regulation 88 pensionannualisation | https://www.bizkaia.eus/documents/880307/15187815/ca_47_2014.pdf#a88 | 1.º Tratándose de pensiones y haberes pasivos, y a los exclusivos efectos de determinar el porcentaje de retención aplicable, procederá la elevación al año de las cantidades | 2026-10-28 | rule |

| 189 | The base / Basque specific employee/pension inputs; LF109 | For all three current regulations, a newly acquired pension/passive-benefit status uses **annualisation** for selecting the rate, with the statutory option not to annualise. Do not apply the older Álava FAQ's one-child pension column to current 2026 law. Sporadic daily manual labour uses **daily pay ×100** for the table forecast. A judicial spouse/partner compensatory pension can reduce the forecast under the specific local rule, with a **2% rate floor**, an indefinite contract, the first eligible tax period being the one after the court fixed it, and the required communication/judgment evidence. Gipuzkoa additionally states the recipient must not cohabit with the payer. Do not confuse a child's maintenance payments with this spouse-pension route. Special fishing, elected impatriate and founder/foreign-tax-credit routes require their separate statutory conditions and are outside this ordinary table method ([B art. 88.3](https://www.bizkaia.eus/documents/880307/15187815/ca_47_2014.pdf), [A art. 84.3](https://web.araba.eus/documents/d/araba/indice-reglamento_irpf_cas-2-pdf), [G art. 100.3](https://www.gipuzkoa.eus/documents/2456431/8.0755457E7/DF%2033-2014%20(2026-2).pdf/7e4c25eb-a139-fa5f-d310-deaa4c4bcffd)). | Own regulation 84 pensionannualisation | https://web.araba.eus/documents/d/araba/indice-reglamento_irpf_cas-2-pdf#a84 | 1ª. | 205 | The base / Basque specific employee/pension inputs; LF109 | For all three current regulations, a newly acquired pension/passive-benefit status uses **annualisation** for selecting the rate, with the statutory option not to annualise. | 258 | The base / Navarra base, disability, minimum and changes; LF147 | For2026, the annual Navarra dependent-income ceiling is **8,400**, and the specified double family-unit ceiling is **16,800**. Income means taxable income including imputations less fiscally deductible expenses, excluding exempt income; do not subtract base reductions or prior-year loss offsets. Exactly8,400 meets this income gate;8,400.01 fails. The authority's [2025 manual, updated in2026, printed pp227/230](https://www.navarra.es/documents/48192/35409067/Manual%2Bte%C3%B3rico%2Bde%2Bla%2Bcampa%C3%B1a%2Bde%2B2025.pdf/c9e11ce5-ae6f-eb9a-040b-265f1f040525?download=true&t=1775726152475) establishes Navarra's actual annual basis. Applying that same basis to2026 follows the unchanged art62.9 reference and unchanged [2026 IPREM values](https://vivienda.castillalamancha.es/iprem), set in [LPGE31/2022 DA90](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2022-22128#da-90). This does not assert that a2026-income Navarra manual has been published. Keep all age, family, disability, support and apportionment conditions; the amount alone does not establish entitlement. | LPGE31/2022 DA90 annualIPREM14payments | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2022-22128#da-90 | d) En los supuestos en que la referencia al salario mínimo interprofesional ha sido sustituida por la referencia al IPREM en aplicación de lo establecido en el Real Decreto-ley 3/2004, de 25 de junio, la cuantía anual del IPREM será de 8.400 euros cuando las correspondientes normas se refieran al salario mínimo interprofesional en cómputo anual, salvo que expresamente excluyeran las pagas extraordinarias; en este caso, la cuantía será de 7.200 euros. | 2026-10-28 | combinedsource annualbasis: 8400 and double16800 follow selected ownNavarraannualdependentbasis; not monthly7200test. |

Apply art. 71's graduation and agreement/payment conditions before any reduction. \| [LF 13/2000 arts. 50/52/68–77](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2001-3494) \| | Own procedure law article 52 supplementary rate | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2001-3494#a52 | c) Una vez transcurridos los citados tres meses pero antes de que lleguen a transcurrir los doce meses siguientes al término del plazo voluntario de presentación e ingreso, se aplicará un recargo del 10 por 100, con exclusión del interés de demora y de las sanciones que, en otro caso, hubieran podido exigirse. | 2026-10-28 | rule |

| 289 | The base / Navarra base, disability, minimum and changes; LF147 | For2026, the annual Navarra dependent-income ceiling is **8,400**, and the specified double family-unit ceiling is **16,800**. Income means taxable income including imputations less fiscally deductible expenses, excluding exempt income; do not subtract base reductions or prior-year loss offsets. Exactly8,400 meets this income gate;8,400.01 fails. The authority's [2025 manual, updated in2026, printed pp227/230](https://www.navarra.es/documents/48192/35409067/Manual%2Bte%C3%B3rico%2Bde%2Bla%2Bcampa%C3%B1a%2Bde%2B2025.pdf/c9e11ce5-ae6f-eb9a-040b-265f1f040525?download=true&t=1775726152475) establishes Navarra's actual annual basis. Applying that same basis to2026 follows the unchanged art62.9 reference and unchanged [2026 IPREM values](https://vivienda.castillalamancha.es/iprem), set in [LPGE31/2022 DA90](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2022-22128#da-90). This does not assert that a2026-income Navarra manual has been published. Keep all age, family, disability, support and apportionment conditions; the amount alone does not establish entitlement. | Official2026 indicator continuation | https://vivienda.castillalamancha.es/iprem | 2026 600,00 € 7.200,00 € 8.400,00 € | 2026-10-28 | rule |

| 26 | [Basque Economic Agreement art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2002-9969#a7) assigns ordinary employment withholding by where work/services are performed. Where common/Basque work is mixed or its location cannot be determined, use the assigned work centre; telework, foreign work and the specified sea-platform work follow that centre. Apply the competent Basque territory's rules, rather than the employee's annual IRPF residence alone. Pensions/public-system benefits generally follow recipient residence. Directors have payer-domicile and joint corporate-tax allocation rules. State public-sector pay has its separate State allocation and listed institutional exceptions. An employee living in Madrid but assigned to a Bilbao centre for telework does not acquire the State payroll algorithm simply from that residence. | Who this applies to; current LF26; exact retained |
| 28 | [Navarra Economic Agreement art. 10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1990-31117#a1-13) similarly assigns ordinary work in Navarra, mixed/indeterminate work by assigned centre and telework/foreign work to that centre. It separately assigns Navarra/local public employees, route transport workers by payer domicile, pensions and directors under their specified limbs, with State public-sector exceptions and compensation provisions. Keep the final annual-residence tax calculation and the withholding administration as separate determinations. Obtain actual work, centre-assignment, payer and residence records. | Who this applies to; current LF28; exact retained |

Do not apply the percentage only to the excess over the lower boundary. | The base / Basque2026 general employee table; current LF42; exact retained |

| 84 | 1. Determine expected **gross annual cash plus valued in-kind pay**, fixed and foreseeable variable pay, for the calendar year from this payer. Include extra pay; apply the jurisdiction's actual non-integration percentages and caps to eligible irregular/benefit income. Exclude only the qualifying employer pension/EPSV and specified RETA payments identified by that regulation. Do not subtract employee social contributions, the State's 2,000 employment expense or its payroll employment reduction before selecting this table. Those are separate annual-tax/payroll rules. | The base / Basque computation, family facts and prospective changes; current LF84; exact retained |
| 85 | 2. Count descendants for whom the applicable NF art. 79 deduction is available, using circumstances on **1 January or employment commencement**. The age gate is not more than 30 in that tax period, except the art. 82 disability/dependency case. Establish cohabitation/statutory maintenance, the child's non-exempt income no more than annual SMI, other-family-unit and own-return exclusions from the annual PIT procedure. A shared parent's annual credit fraction does not turn a qualifying child into half a child in the table. Keep signed family declarations; absent communication apply the rate without the uncommunicated facts. | The base / Basque computation, family facts and prospective changes; current LF85; exact retained |
| 86 | 3. Select the one percentage for annual forecast and descendant column, then apply the active-disability point reduction below and the applicable statutory minimum. The ordinary percentage applies to the **whole relevant payment**, including the integration-adjusted amount where the statute requires it. In-kind plus cash has the local combined-withholding/ingreso-a-cuenta treatment; the Basque regulations charge the resulting aggregate against cash. Retain the tax certificate/in-kind ledger rather than applying the common-territory forecast exclusions by analogy. | The base / Basque computation, family facts and prospective changes; current LF86; exact retained |
| 87 | 4. Where remuneration changes in-year, recalculate the table percentage using the changed annual forecast; apply it **prospectively from the change**. Contract continuation/re-engagement within the same calendar year counts both already paid and expected further pay. These provisions do not direct the State quota-minus-prior-withholding divided by remaining-pay algorithm. Preserve the old and new annual forecasts, table row, rate and effective payment. | The base / Basque computation, family facts and prospective changes; current LF87; exact retained |
| 88 | 5. A relationship **under one year** has a **2% minimum**; other special dependent relationships generally have **15%**, with the statutory prisoner/disability exceptions. Gipuzkoa expressly includes the specified artists/technical-assistant relationship in the 2% floor; do not infer an identical special-artists override from Bizkaia/Álava wording. Part-time employment alone is not stated as the Basque 2% trigger. A requested higher rate must be written, timely for payroll and retained under the jurisdiction's continuation/change rules. | The base / Basque computation, family facts and prospective changes; current LF88; exact retained |
| 90 | Sources for these methods: [Bizkaia arts. 87–88/107](https://www.bizkaia.eus/documents/880307/15187815/ca_47_2014.pdf), [Álava arts. 83–85](https://web.araba.eus/documents/d/araba/indice-reglamento_irpf_cas-2-pdf), [Gipuzkoa arts. 99–101](https://www.gipuzkoa.eus/documents/2456431/8.0755457E7/DF%2033-2014%20(2026-2).pdf/7e4c25eb-a139-fa5f-d310-deaa4c4bcffd). The employee's annual tax remains the separate foral PIT computation. | The base / Basque computation, family facts and prospective changes; current LF90; exact retained |
| 94 | Category (a) is recognised disability **at least 33% and below 65%**. Category (b) has that degree plus the required reduced mobility, currently BLAM final mobility limitation **at least 25%** under RD 888/2022; category (c) is **at least 65%**. Apply the table's category, not both columns together. These are **percentage points removed from the table rate**, floored at zero subject to applicable statutory minimum-rate rules; they are not a deduction of that percentage from tax money. Obtain official status evidence and any applicable historical-certificate transition rather than replacing it with a guess. | The base / Basque active-disability point reductions; current LF94; exact retained |

| 109 | For all three current regulations, a newly acquired pension/passive-benefit status uses **annualisation** for selecting the rate, with the statutory option not to annualise. Do not apply the older Álava FAQ's one-child pension column to current 2026 law. Sporadic daily manual labour uses **daily pay ×100** for the table forecast. A judicial spouse/partner compensatory pension can reduce the forecast under the specific local rule, with a **2% rate floor**, an indefinite contract, the first eligible tax period being the one after the court fixed it, and the required communication/judgment evidence. Gipuzkoa additionally states the recipient must not cohabit with the payer. Do not confuse a child's maintenance payments with this spouse-pension route. Special fishing, elected impatriate and founder/foreign-tax-credit routes require their separate statutory conditions and are outside this ordinary table method ([B art. 88.3](https://www.bizkaia.eus/documents/880307/15187815/ca_47_2014.pdf), [A art. 84.3](https://web.araba.eus/documents/d/araba/indice-reglamento_irpf_cas-2-pdf), [G art. 100.3](https://www.gipuzkoa.eus/documents/2456431/8.0755457E7/DF%2033-2014%20(2026-2).pdf/7e4c25eb-a139-fa5f-d310-deaa4c4bcffd)). | The base / Basque specific employee/pension inputs; current LF109; exact retained |

Below, choose the **highest threshold strictly exceeded**; at exactly a threshold the preceding row still applies. | 156 | The **2% minimum** applies to relationships under one year **or incomplete working time**, and the specified artists/technical relationship; other special dependent relationships have **15%**, subject to the prisoner/disability exceptions. Navarra's ordinary part-time worker can therefore need 2% despite a zero table result. Directors receive **35%** under art. 70.b, rather than the ordinary employee table. | The base / Navarra base, disability, minimum and changes; current LF160; exact retained |
| 158 | Apply the selected rate to the whole relevant payment. Regularise for continuation/re-engagement, resumption/change of the specified unemployment benefit, or general-rule/collective-agreement pay changes, using paid plus forecast further remuneration for the same year and applying the new rate prospectively. A category-related pay reduction permits a worker's written request for a new rate under art. 72.4. These are Navarra's actual routes, not the State AEAT balancing algorithm. The initial family facts are defined at 1 January/commencement; do not invent an automatic mid-year family recalculation unsupported by art. 71. | The base / Navarra base, disability, minimum and changes; current LF162; exact retained |
| 160 | For passive benefits under art. 14.2.a, Navarra uses the **one-child column** and annualises a newly acquired pension status; the taxpayer can opt for the general table using actual personal circumstances. Sporadic daily manual work uses **daily pay ×100**. A communicated judicial spouse compensatory pension reduces the forecast, subject to a **2% rate floor**; Navarra's art. 71.Tres.3 does not state the Basque indefinite-contract/following-year gateway. Eligible art. 17.2 irregular-income reductions change the payment base under art. 71.Tres.4. Preserve written personal evidence and higher-rate requests under art. 72 ([DF 174/1999 arts. 70–72](https://www.navarra.es/es/web/normativa-hacienda/-/reglamento-del-impuesto-sobre-la-renta-de-las-personas-f%C3%ADsicas-df-174/1999-de-24-de-mayo-?print=true)). | The base / Navarra base, disability, minimum and changes; current LF164; exact retained |
| 164 | These are statutory ordinary windows; apply actual holidays, weekend extensions, direct-debit cutoffs and accepted-filing/payment receipts to the period. Monthly status is the actual statutory VAT/public-payer condition, not employee headcount. | Deadlines and filing; current LF168; exact retained |

| 173 | For Bizkaia/Gipuzkoa/Navarra, a subject payment producing zero withholding can require a negative return, while no subject payments does not under the cited general rules. Álava's current art104 is expressly different. Separate paid-income/withholding aggregation from accrued contribution settlement. A payroll certificate and the annual190 must tie to actual payments, correctly valued in-kind amounts and local income-integration fields. Foral penalty/surcharge law is administration-specific; do not copy the State LGT numbers onto a foral assessment without the corresponding provincial/Navarra authority. | Deadlines and filing; current LF179; exact retained |
| 177 | Use the procedure law of the administration assessing the liability. Voluntary correction before a qualifying prior request, an underpayment offence and enforced collection are separate events. Interest uses the applicable period's statutory rate and actual accrual days; do not transplant the common-territory monthly surcharge into a foral return. | Penalties and interest; current LF183; exact retained |
| 179 | \| Administration \| Voluntary late correction and collection \| Underpayment offence before conditional reductions \| Primary law \| | Penalties and interest; current LF185; exact retained |
| 181 | \| Bizkaia \| Art. 27's **declaration-based assessment** surcharge is 5% of assessed payable quota, or 0.5% if the declaration is filed within the next three working days. It preserves the art. 203 exception. Under art. 26.7, late **self-assessments** accrue interest from the original deadline except when both voluntary filing and payment occur within six months. Art. 28 collection is 5%, reduced to 0.5% for full effective payment in the first three working days of the executive period; 20% applies after the notified enforcement order's extraordinary payment term expires. The two collection surcharges are incompatible. \| Art. 196:100–250% of unpaid tax for a culpable self-assessment underpayment; qualifying voluntary regularisation is excluded. Art. 203 ordinary late-return offence:400, with separate census/information amounts and narrowly specified no-request reductions. Concealment's debt incidence must exceed10%. | 186 | For Álava/Gipuzkoa, filing an unpaid late self-assessment without the prescribed contemporaneous deferral, instalment or compensation request does not prevent executive collection costs. For all four, preserve notice dates, the exact declaration/self-assessment route, payment evidence, correction period and any reduction conditions. Incorrect refunds, false invoices, information omissions and failure to provide an assessment declaration have their own offences. A percentage range is not an automatic assessment: establish culpability, statutory base, classification and applicable reductions under the cited law. | Penalties and interest; current LF192; exact retained |
| 190 | Wage-withholding allocation follows the actual Concierto/Convenio points of connection described above. It is separate from final annual IRPF residence. A nonresident worker, treaty-exempt work or elected specialist impatriate regime needs the corresponding substantive law before replacing an ordinary percentage. Keep foreign tax and social-insurance certificates separately; wage withholding does not settle social contributions. | Cross-border points; current LF196; exact retained |
| 194 | **Basque ordinary and disability control.** Full-year ordinary indefinite employee with30,000 forecast, two qualifying descendants and2,500 monthly taxable cash: table13%, withholding325. With category(a) active disability the reduction is7points, producing6%, withholding150. This does not reduce the base by social contributions or use the State employment reduction. | Two worked examples that recompute; current LF200; exact retained |
| 213 | **Navarra boundary and part-time control.** At30,000 forecast and two qualifying children, choose the threshold27,500 row (12.6%), giving315 on2,500 payment. At exactly30,250 the same12.6% row still applies; at30,250.01 the rate becomes13.7%. A separate lawful indefinite part-time employment with16,000 forecast and1,000 payment has zero from the general table but the2% incomplete-time floor produces20 withheld. | Two worked examples that recompute; current LF219; exact retained |

## Frozen reservations retained verbatim
**Not yet confirmed:** historical disability-certificate transition for a particular award; specialist impatriate/founder/fishing, unusual-benefit and cross-year arrears methods; the specified historical disability and specialist benefit facts require their applicable provision before use. The ordinary Navarra dependent-income basis is supplied above, with its dated-source provenance. Final annual tax is covered by the separate foral income-tax Guide; no accountant attestation is claimed.
